### Table 2 — Total constructors
| Constructor | Typed domain | Complete canonical output mapping | Invalid tuple exclusion |
|---|---|---|---|
| `C_IDENTITY` | Exact input keys `binding`, `generation`, `method`, `profile`, `request_identity`, `transaction`; every value is the explicitly supplied typed record. | **Emitted:** all six inputs, verbatim, under exactly the sorted output keys `binding,generation,method,profile,request_identity,transaction`. **Validation-only:** none. No value is derived, normalized, renamed, or dropped. | Accept iff the input key set is exactly those six keys and each value satisfies its declared Table-1 explicit type; reject every omission, extra key, or type failure. No additional cross-field equality or transformation is implicit. |
| `C_LEDGER` | Exact input keys `callbacks`, `diagnostics`, `external_bytes`, `leases`, `reservations`, `transport_bytes`, `transport_messages`, `terminal_before`, `terminal_after`; every value is explicitly supplied. | **Emitted:** the seven dimension inputs verbatim under exactly the sorted `dimensions` keys `callbacks,diagnostics,external_bytes,leases,reservations,transport_bytes,transport_messages`, and `terminal_before`/`terminal_after` verbatim as exactly `terminal.before`/`terminal.after`; the complete sorted output key paths are `dimensions` then `terminal`, with `after` then `before` inside `terminal`. **Validation-only:** none. | Accept iff the input key set is exactly those nine keys; each dimension has exactly `after,available,before,delta,known_zero`; an available dimension has integer `before`, `after`, and `delta`, `after-before=delta`, and `known_zero=(delta=0)`; an unavailable dimension has `before=after=delta=null` and `known_zero=false`; each terminal value has exactly `available,value`, with non-null string `value` iff available and null otherwise. Reject all other tuples. |
| `C_RECORD` | Exact common input keys `kind,name,identity,frames,result,error,terminal,external,accounting,transport,commitments,lifecycle,round_trip_ledger,refusal`, plus the kind-specific keys below. | **Emitted:** `name` and every supplied typed record except `kind`, verbatim. Exact sorted output keys are: world = `accounting,commitments,comparison,concurrency,control,error,external,frames,identity,lifecycle,name,refusal,result,round_trip_ledger,terminal,transport`; worker = `accounting,commitments,error,external,frames,identity,lifecycle,name,refusal,result,round_trip_ledger,terminal,transport`; execution = `accounting,commitments,control,error,external,frames,identity,lifecycle,name,refusal,result,round_trip_ledger,terminal,transport`. **Validation-only discriminator:** `kind`; it is never emitted. No supplied input is otherwise dropped, derived, normalized, renamed, or transformed. | Accept iff the input key set equals the common set plus `control,comparison,concurrency` for `kind="world"`, plus no keys for `kind="worker"`, or plus `control` for `kind="execution"`; `name` is respectively exactly `world-NN`, `worker-NNN`, or `execution-NNN` for the corresponding enumerated Table-4 row; every reference resolves to the declared typed Table-1 value; and the emitted object equals that row's Table-3 canonical value. Reject every other kind, omission, extra key, unresolved/wrong-typed reference, name/category mismatch, or noncanonical result. The discriminator selects validation only, never canned semantic values. |
| `C_WORLD` | Exact input keys `name,provenance,record,root_identity`. `record` names the exact Table-3 `CompleteRecord`; `provenance` has exact keys `schema,source_head`. | **Emitted:** exactly the sorted root keys `provenance,record,root_identity`. `provenance` is emitted verbatim as the two literal identifier strings under sorted keys `schema,source_head` (the identifiers are not dereferenced); `record` is replaced by the exact canonical Table-3 object it names; `root_identity` is emitted verbatim. **Validation-only:** `name`; it is never emitted. No other drop, lookup, derivation, normalization, rename, or transform occurs. | Accept iff the input key set is exactly `name,provenance,record,root_identity`; the invocation is one of the 25 enumerated Table-4 roots; `name=root_identity=record.name`; `record` resolves to the corresponding world-kind `CORE_NN`; and `provenance` is exactly `{"schema":"A_SCHEMA","source_head":"A_HEAD"}`. Reject every other tuple, including dereferenced provenance, unknown/extra keys, mismatch, omission, or ambient/default value. |

These partitions are exhaustive: every supplied constructor input is either emitted at the stated exact path or named validation-only with an exact predicate. Canonical serialization then applies the document-wide UTF-8, sorted-key, compact-separator, one-LF rule. Table-3 and Table-4 lengths and hashes were regenerated under this literal mapping and are unchanged because the corrected contract now describes their existing bytes.

## Accepted semantic policy and required contrasts

- Refusal policy: Layer-1 refusal is externally transparent and is represented only by `refusal`. Worlds 17–23 therefore use `error.present=false`, class `none`, null code/message/data, canonical serialized `null`, while retaining exact external request/response and transport observations.
- World 3 has an exact top-level JSON-RPC `result:null` response and `Result.form=null` with exact bytes `null`; worlds 4 and 5 contain distinct independently fixed nonempty incoming/outgoing literals and therefore distinct body/frame lengths and hashes.
- Worlds 6–11 have pairwise-distinct accepted-input literals and coherent protocol, malformed, cancellation, deadline, shutdown, and transport-unavailable errors, including exact class/code/message/data/serialization, result unavailability, terminal, frames, external/transport measures, lifecycle, accounting, and all seven ledger dimensions.
- Worlds 12–14 distinguish unavailable nil request payload, available zero-length payload, and available nonempty payload, including payload bytes/length/hash and exact frames/results.
- World 15 records immutable source and retained pre/post value+digest pairs; the source changes while retained bytes/value/digest do not, and `no_alias=true`.
- World 24 names exactly 16 complete workers. World 25 names exactly 22 executions in 11 pairs. Each enabled execution copies explicit semantic atoms; each disabled execution marks internal observation fields unavailable while preserving exactly equal external and transport records.
- Constructor review is semantic: `C_RECORD` and `C_WORLD` have no canned outputs and no behavior selected merely by `enabled`, world name, record name, kind, or scenario labels.

## Disposable author self-check

A fresh disposable validator literally executed the four Table-2 mappings and their total rejection predicates: it partitioned every supplied input into emitted or validation-only, emitted `C_RECORD.kind` nowhere, emitted `C_WORLD.name` nowhere, retained literal provenance identifiers without dereference, expanded only the named Table-3 record at `C_WORLD.record`, and serialized by the document-wide canonical rule. It reproduced every Table-3 record and all 25 Table-4 roots byte-for-byte and rechecked every published length and SHA-256. A separate fixed-point expansion cross-check retained the prior algorithm-independence check. The checks also covered exact four-table count, 25 roots, 16 workers, 22 executions, closure, schemas, all semantic contrasts above, refusal coherence, neutral constructors, pairwise error distinction, immutable mutation proof, enabled/disabled external equality, and explicit World 12 request-payload-unavailable/result-null/response-null/terminal/external/request-scoped-ledger consistency distinct from World 13 available-zero-length/result-empty semantics.

```text
Table2 literal mapping records reproduced=63/63; roots reproduced=25/25; world roots=25; W24 workers=16; W25 executions=22; unaccounted constructor inputs=0; emitted/validation-only overlap=0; undefined refs=0; cycles=0; partial constructors=0; missing fields=0; unexpected fields=0; canonical defects=0; byte mismatches=0; length mismatches=0; SHA-256 mismatches=0; fixed-point mismatches=0; semantic contrast defects=0; World12 consistency defects=0.
```

The fresh validator and independent expansion cross-check reported all defect counts zero. This is an author self-check only, not reviewer authority. All three reviews remain blocked, qualification remains pending, and **no GO is issued**.
